6     BYD ELECTRONIC (INTERNATIONAL) COMPANY LIMITED • ANNUAL REPORT 2020
                                                                          CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT
                                                                                            主席報告書


Facing the COVID-19 outbreak in 2020, the Group undertook                 二零二零年新冠疫情爆發，集團出於社會責任
its social responsibilities and stepped forward to launch PPEs,           的承擔，率先推出防護產品，極大地緩解醫療
which significantly mitigated medical protective supply shortages         防護物資的短缺，同時也為集團的收入和利潤
and contributed to the Group’s revenue and profits. Following the         帶來正面貢獻。疫情過後，國家有望加強醫療
pandemic, China is expected to input more in health and medical           衛生領域的投入，提升社會應對突發公共衛生
care with the aim of enhancing public response capacities to public       事件的能力，而中國醫療器械市場規模已是過
health emergencies. The medical instrument market has witnessed a         萬億的終端採購規模，而且呈現出穩定增長態
terminal purchase size of over RMB1 trillion and has shown a steady       勢。集團將結合自身精密製造技術及產品研發
growth trend. The Group, in the combination of its own precision          的優勢，進一步開拓醫療健康業務，為未來的
manufacturing technology and product R&D strengths, will further          發展培養新一股中堅力量。
explore the health and medical care business, developing a new
backbone force for its future development.


Looking forward to 2021, with the rolling out and extensive application   展望二零二一年，隨著有效疫苗陸續面世及全
of effective vaccines, the market widely expects that the global          面應用後，市場普遍預期環球經濟可望從低谷
economy will rebound and improve over time. However, with the             反彈，並逐步改善，但由於宏觀局勢仍持續緊
tensing general landscape, there are still many uncertainties, which      張，存在諸多不確定因素為全球經濟增長帶來
may put global economic growth at risk. As 5G and AI technology           重大挑戰。隨著 5G 及人工智能技術的不斷推
progress, the era of interconnection of all is approaching and new        進，萬物互聯的時代臨近，新的業務模式亦將
business models will appear. At the forefront of industry change, BYD     會開啟。身處行業變革的前沿，比亞迪電子將
Electronic will actively seize the opportunities of the era, continue     積極把握時代機遇，持續發揮積累多年的核心
to stretch its strengths accumulated over years in core resource          資源平台優勢，不斷提升與客戶戰略合作的深
platforms, and continuously expand and deepen strategic cooperation       度和廣度，以實現四大業務板塊的良好發展，
with customers, so as to secure sound development of three major          帶動業務實現新的成長和突破。
business segments and drive its business into a new growth cycle.


In conclusion, I, on behalf of BYD Electronic, would like to thank our    最後，本人謹代表比亞迪電子感謝一直支援我
customers, business partners, investors, and shareholders for their       們的客戶、業務夥伴、投資者及股東，並衷心
long support, as well as all of our staff for their contributions and     感謝全體員工在過去一年的付出和貢獻。
efforts over the past year.




Wang Chuan-fu                                                             主席

MANAGEMENT DISCUSSION AND ANALYSIS
管理層討論與分析

A1.1 Types of Emissions and Respective Emissions Data                        A1.1 排放物種類及相關排放數據
     Waste emissions include waste water, waste gas, solid wastes              排放物的種類有廢水、廢氣、固體廢棄
     and noise. Waste water can be further divided into production             物、噪聲。廢水有生產廢水和生活污水；

facilities and main constructions are designed, constructed and                確保環境風險受控。此外，比公司建立了一
put into production/operations simultaneously, so as to ensure that            體化的環境管理體系 ( E M S ) 並於 2003 年獲得
environmental risks are under control. In addition, the Company has            ISO14001體系認證，在2018年10月份進行了
established an integrated environmental management system (EMS)                換版體系認證。
and obtained the ISO14001 certification for the system in 2003, and

Forbiddance of Child Labor                                                   嚴禁僱傭童工
In order to effectively safeguard the basic rights of employees and          為了切實維護員工的各項基本權利、杜絕聘用
eliminate the employment of child labor, the Group has formulated            童工的現象，本集團根據《中華人民共和國勞
relevant rules and regulations to prohibit child labor in accordance         動法》、《未成年人保護法》以及《禁止使用童工
with the Labor Law of the People’s Republic of China, the Law on the         規定》等法律法規，制定了相應的規章制度，
Protection of Minors and the Rules on the Forbiddance of Child Labor.        嚴禁聘用童工。


By continuously improving and optimizing its recruitment procedure,          本集團通過不斷完善及優化招聘流程，全面貫
the Group fully implemented its corporate social responsibilities            徹落實企業社會責任，保護兒童的健康成長。
and ensured the healthy growth of children. On the one hand, the             一方面積極對外進行宣傳，向社會公開關於

active external publicity. On the other hand, the Group has actively         關規定，明確招聘條件，建立身份識別體系，
implemented the rules by specifying the terms of employment and              制定童工救助措施。
formulating the identification system and relief measures for child labor.

Supply Chain Platform                                                           供應鏈平台
 In recent years, the Group has transitioned from price-based                   近幾年，本集團對於生產性物料的採購已經從

 Some important parts and components are procured from leading                  名領先的供應商，側重風險和策略管理。本集
 suppliers in the industry, focusing on risk and strategic management.          團繼續大力推行陽光採購和綠色採購，通過對
 The Group has vigorously pushed forward sunshine procurement and               供應商的生命周期管理形成閉環，打造高效協
 green procurement, and created an efficient, collaborative, mutually           同、互利共贏的供應鏈平台。
 beneficial supply chain platform by forming a closed loop of the life

and other one-stop services. Its business covers smart phones,           網、智能家居、遊戲硬件、機器人、無人機、
smart wearables, computers, automotive intelligent systems, Internet     通信設備、電子霧化、醫療器械等廣闊的市場
of Things, smart home, game hardware, robots, unmanned aerial            領域。依託於業界領先的研發和製造實力、多
vehicles, communication equipment, electronic atomization, medical       元的產品組合、以及優質的客戶資源，集團業
devices and other broad market areas. Leveraging on its industry-        務已經進入新一輪的高速增長週期。
leading R&D and manufacturing capabilities, diversified product

 2.1 BASIS OF PREPARATION                                                      2.1 編製基準
      These financial statements have been prepared in accordance                此等財務報表乃按照由香港會計師公會
      with Hong Kong Financial Reporting Standards (“HKFRSs”) (which            （「香港會計師公會」）頒佈的《香港財務報
      include all Hong Kong Financial Reporting Standards, Hong Kong             告準則》
                                                                                    （「《香港財務報告準則》」）
                                                                                                 （包括所
      Accounting Standards (“HKASs”) and Interpretations) issued by the          有《香港財務報告準則》、《香港會計準則》
      Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public Accountants (“HKICPA”),           （「《香港會計準則》」）及詮釋）、香港公認會
      accounting principles generally accepted in Hong Kong and the              計原則及香港公司條例而編製。此等財務
      Hong Kong Companies Ordinance. They have been prepared                     報表乃按照歷史成本法編製，惟若干指定
      under the historical cost convention, except for certain equity            為以公允價值計量並計入其他綜合收益的

  (b) Amendments to HKFRS 3 clarify and provide additional               (b) 《香港財務報告準則》第3號的修訂釐清
     guidance on the definition of a business. The amendments               並規定有關業務的定義之額外指引。
     clarify that for an integrated set of activities and assets to         該等修訂釐清，倘一系列綜合活動和
     be considered a business, it must include, at a minimum, an            資產要被視為業務，其須至少包括一
     input and a substantive process that together significantly            項投入及一個實質性過程，兩者共同
     contribute to the ability to create output. A business can exist       對創造產出的能力作出重大貢獻。一
     without including all of the inputs and processes needed to            項業務可不必包括所有創造產量所需
     create outputs. The amendments remove the assessment                   的投入與過程而存在。該等修訂移除
     of whether market participants are capable of acquiring the            對市場參與者是否有能力獲得該業務
     business and continue to produce outputs. Instead, the                 並持續產出之評估，反之，重點聚
     focus is on whether acquired inputs and acquired substantive           焦於獲得的投入及實質性過程是否共
     processes together significantly contribute to the ability to          同對創造產量的能力作出重大貢獻。
     create outputs. The amendments have also narrowed the                  該等修正亦縮小產出的定義，重點聚
     definition of outputs to focus on goods or services provided to        焦於向客戶提供的商品或服務、投資
     customers, investment income or other income from ordinary             收入或來自日常活動的其他收入。此
     activities. Furthermore, the amendments provide guidance               外，該等修訂規定評估收購流程是否
     to assess whether an acquired process is substantive and               具有實質性的指引，並引入可選的公
     introduce an optional fair value concentration test to permit a        允價值集中測試，以便簡化評估所
     simplified assessment of whether an acquired set of activities         獲得的一系列活動及資產是否為非業
     and assets is not a business. The Group has applied the                務。本集團將該等修訂追溯應用於二

 2.4 SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING                                        2.4 重要會計政策摘要
     POLICIES
      BUSINESS COMBINATIONS                                                    業務合併
      Business combinations are accounted for using the acquisition            業務合併乃以收購法入賬。轉讓的代價乃
      method. The consideration transferred is measured at the                 以收購日期的公允價值計算，該公允價值
      acquisition date fair value which is the sum of the acquisition          為本集團轉讓的資產、本集團自被收購方
      date fair values of assets transferred by the Group, liabilities         的前擁有人承擔的負債及本集團發行以換
      assumed by the Group to the former owners of the acquiree and            取被收購方控制權的股本權益於收購日期
      the equity interests issued by the Group in exchange for control         的公允價值總和。於各業務合併中，本集
      of the acquiree. For each business combination, the Group elects         團選擇是否以公允價值或被收購方可識別
      whether to measure the non-controlling interests in the acquiree         資產淨值的應佔比例，計量於被收購方的
      that are present ownership interests and entitle their holders to a      非控股權益（屬現時擁有人權益並賦予擁有
      proportionate share of net assets in the event of liquidation at fair    人權利於清盤時按比例分佔其資產淨值）。
      value or at the proportionate share of the acquiree’s identifiable       非控股權益的所有其他組成部分乃以公允
      net assets. All other components of non-controlling interests are        價值計量。收購相關成本於產生時列為開
      measured at fair value. Acquisition-related costs are expensed as        支。
      incurred.


      The Group determines that it has acquired a business when the            當所購入的一系列業務及資產包括一項投
      acquired set of activities and assets includes an input and a            入及一項實質性流程，共同對創造產出的
      substantive process that together significantly contribute to the        能力作出重大貢獻時，本集團確定其已收
      ability to create outputs.                                               購一項業務。


      When the Group acquires a business, it assesses the financial            當本集團收購一項業務時，會根據合同條
      assets and liabilities assumed for appropriate classification and        款、於收購日期的經濟環境及相關條件，
      designation in accordance with the contractual terms, economic           評估將承擔的金融資產及負債，以作出適
      circumstances and pertinent conditions as at the acquisition date.       合的分類及指定，其中包括將被收購方主
      This includes the separation of embedded derivatives in host             合同中的嵌入式衍生工具進行分離。
      contracts of the acquiree.


      If the business combination is achieved in stages, the previously        倘業務合併分階段進行，先前持有的股權
      held equity interest is remeasured at its acquisition date fair value    按收購日期的公允價值重新計量，而任何
      and any resulting gain or loss is recognised in profit or loss.          所得收益或虧損則於損益中確認。


      Any contingent consideration to be transferred by the acquirer           收購方所轉讓的任何或然代價按於收購日
      is recognised at fair value at the acquisition date. Contingent          期的公允價值確認。分類為資產或負債的
      consideration classified as an asset or liability is measured            或然代價以公允價值計量，公允價值變動
      at fair value with changes in fair value recognised in profit or         確認於損益內。分類為權益的或然代價並
      loss. Contingent consideration that is classified as equity is not       無重新計量，而其後結算於權益中入賬。
      remeasured and subsequent settlement is accounted for within
      equity.

    POLICIES (Continued)
  FAIR VALUE MEASUREMENT                                                   公允價值計量
  The Group measures its listed equity investments and derivative          本集團於各報告期末以公允價值計量其上
  financial instruments at fair value at the end of each reporting         市股本投資及衍生金融工具。公允價值指
  period. Fair value is the price that would be received to sell           市場參與者於計量日期在有序交易中出售
  an asset or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction       資產所收取或轉讓負債所支付的價格。公
  between market participants at the measurement date. The                 允價值計量所依據的假設為，出售資產或
  fair value measurement is based on the presumption that the              轉讓負債的交易乃於該資產或負債的主要
  transaction to sell the asset or transfer the liability takes place      市場進行，或如無主要市場，則於對該資
  either in the principal market for the asset or liability, or in the     產或負債最為有利的市場進行。本集團必
  absence of a principal market, in the most advantageous market           須可進入該主要或最為有利的市場。計量
  for the asset or liability. The principal or the most advantageous       資產或負債的公允價值使用市場參與者於
  market must be accessible by the Group. The fair value of an             為該資產或負債定價時所依據的假設，即
  asset or a liability is measured using the assumptions that market       假設市場參與者按其最佳經濟利益行事。
  participants would use when pricing the asset or liability, assuming
  that market participants act in their economic best interest.


  A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into             非金融資產公允價值的計量參考市場參與
  account a market participant’s ability to generate economic              者可從使用該資產得到的最高及最佳效
  benefits by using the asset in its highest and best use or by selling    用，或把該資產售予另一可從使用該資產
  it to another market participant that would use the asset in its         得到最高及最佳效用的市場參與者所產生
  highest and best use.                                                    的經濟效益。


  The Group uses valuation techniques that are appropriate in              本集團使用適用於不同情況的估值方法，
  the circumstances and for which sufficient data are available to         而其有足夠數據計量公允價值，並盡量利
  measure fair value, maximising the use of relevant observable            用相關可觀察輸入數據及盡量減少使用不
  inputs and minimising the use of unobservable inputs.                    可觀察輸入數據。


  All assets and liabilities for which fair value is measured or           於財務報表中以公允價值計量或披露的所
  disclosed in the financial statements are categorised within the fair    有資產及負債，均以對整體公允價值計量
  value hierarchy, described as follows, based on the lowest level         屬重要的最低水平輸入數據為基礎以公允
  input that is significant to the fair value measurement as a whole:      價值等級分類如下：


  Level 1 – based on quoted prices (unadjusted) in active markets          第一級 － 以相同資產或負債的活躍市場報
            for identical assets or liabilities                                   價（未經調整）為基礎進行計量


  Level 2 – based on valuation techniques for which the lowest level       第二級 － 根據估值方法（對公允價值計量
            input that is significant to the fair value measurement is            屬重要的最低水平輸入數據可直
            observable, either directly or indirectly                             接或間接觀察）進行計量


  Level 3 – based on valuation techniques for which the lowest level       第三級 － 根據估值方法（對公允價值計量
            input that is significant to the fair value measurement is            屬重要的最低水平輸入數據不可
            unobservable                                                          觀察）進行計量

  LEASES                                                                     租賃
  The Group assesses at contract inception whether a contract                本集團於合同開始時評估合同是否屬於或
  is, or contains, a lease. A contract is, or contains, a lease if the       包含租賃。倘合同為換取代價而給予可在
  contract conveys the right to control the use of an identified asset       一段時間內控制使用可識別資產的權利，
  for a period of time in exchange for consideration.                        則該合同屬於或包含租賃。


  Group as a lessee                                                          本集團作為承租人
  The Group applies a single recognition and measurement                     本集團就所有租賃採用單一確認及計量方
  approach for all leases, except for short-term leases and leases of        法，惟短期租賃及低價值資產租賃除外。
  low-value assets. The Group recognises lease liabilities to make           本集團確認租賃付款的租賃負債及使用權
  lease payments and right-of-use assets representing the right to           資產（即使用相關資產的權利）。
  use the underlying assets.


  (a) Right-of-use assets                                                    (a) 使用權資產
      Right-of-use assets are recognised at the commencement                    於租賃開始日期（即相關資產可供使用
      date of the lease (that is the date the underlying asset is               的日期）確認使用權資產。使用權資產
      available for use). Right-of-use assets are measured at cost,             按成本計量，扣除任何累計折舊及任
      less any accumulated depreciation and any impairment                      何減值虧損，並就重新計量租賃負債
      losses, and adjusted for any remeasurement of lease liabilities.          作出調整。使用權資產的成本包括已
      The cost of right-of-use assets includes the amount of lease              確認的租賃負債金額、已發生的初始
      liabilities recognised, initial direct costs incurred, and lease          直接成本、以及於開始日期或之前作
      payments made at or before the commencement date less any                 出的租賃款項付款減已收取的租賃優
      lease incentives received. Right-of-use assets are depreciated            惠。使用權資產按直線基準於租賃期
      on a straight-line basis over the shorter of the lease terms and          及該資產的估計可使用年期兩者中較
      the estimated useful lives of the assets as follows:                      短者折舊如下：


      Leasehold land                                    44 to 99 years          租賃土地               44至99年
      Buildings                                          1 to 20 years          樓宇                  1至20年

      If ownership of the leased asset transfers to the Group by                倘租賃資產的所有權於租賃期結束前

      estimated useful life of the asset.                                       年期計算。

 2.4 SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING                                        2.4 重要會計政策摘要（續）

      INVESTMENTS AND OTHER FINANCIAL ASSETS                                   投資及其他金融資產
      Initial recognition and measurement                                      初步確認及計量
      Financial assets are classified, at initial recognition, as              金融資產於初步確認時分類為其後以攤銷
      subsequently measured at amortised cost, fair value through other        成本、以公允價值計量並計入其他綜合收
      comprehensive income, and fair value through profit or loss.             益及以公允價值計量並計入損益計量。


      The classification of financial assets at initial recognition depends    金融資產於初始確認時之分類乃視乎該等
      on the financial asset’s contractual cash flow characteristics           金融資產的合同現金流量特點及本集團管
      and the Group’s business model for managing them. With the               理該等金融資產的業務模式而定。除並不
      exception of trade receivables that do not contain a significant         包含重大融資成分或本集團已就此應用不
      financing component or for which the Group has applied the               調整重大融資成分影響的實際權宜方法之
      practical expedient of not adjusting the effect of a significant         應收貿易賬款外，本集團初始以公允價值
      financing component, the Group initially measures a financial asset      加上（倘金融資產並非以公允價值計量並計
      at its fair value, plus in the case of a financial asset not at fair     入損益）交易成本計量金融資產。並不包含
      value through profit or loss, transaction costs. Trade receivables       重大融資成分或本集團已應用實際權宜方
      that do not contain a significant financing component or for which       法之應收貿易賬款，乃按根據《香港財務報
      the Group has applied the practical expedient are measured at the        告準則》第15 號按下文「收入確認」所載政
      transaction price determined under HKFRS 15 in accordance with           策釐定之交易價格計量。
      the policies set out for “Revenue recognition” below.


      In order for a financial asset to be classified and measured at          為使金融資產以攤銷成本或以公允價值計
      amortised cost or fair value through other comprehensive income,         量並計入其他綜合收益進行分類及計量，
      it needs to give rise to cash flows that are solely payments of          需產生僅為支付本金及未償還本金利息
      principal and interest (“SPPI”) on the principal amount outstanding.     （「SPPI」）的現金流量。現金流量並非SPPI
      Financial assets with cash flows that are not SPPI are classified        的金融資產以公允價值計量並計入損益分
      and measured at fair value through profit or loss, irrespective of       類及計量，不論其業務模式如何。
      the business model.


      The Group’s business model for managing financial assets refers          本集團管理金融資產的業務模式指其管理
      to how it manages its financial assets in order to generate cash         其金融資產以產生現金流量的方式。業務
      flows. The business model determines whether cash flows will             模式釐定現金流量是否來自收集合同現金
      result from collecting contractual cash flows, selling the financial     流量、出售金融資產，或兩者兼有。以攤
      assets, or both. Financial assets classified and measured at             銷成本分類及計量的金融資產於旨在持有
      amortised cost are held within a business model with the objective       金融資產於以收取合同現金流量的業務模
      to hold financial assets in order to collect contractual cash flows,     式中持有，而以公允價值分類及計量並計
      while financial assets classified and measured at fair value through     入其他綜合收益的金融資產於旨在持有以
      other comprehensive income are held within a business model              收取合同現金流量及銷售的的業務模式中
      with the objective of both holding to collect contractual cash           持有。並非於上述業務模式持有的金融資
      flows and selling. Financial assets which are not held within the        產以公允價值分類及計量並計入損益。
      aforementioned business models are classified and measured at
      fair value through profit or loss.

  When the Group has transferred its rights to receive cash flows          倘若已轉讓其自資產收取現金流量的權利

  it evaluates if, and to what extent, it has retained the risk and        留該資產所有權的風險及回報以及其程

  nor retained substantially all the risks and rewards of the asset        風險及回報，亦無轉讓該資產的控制權，

  recognise the transferred asset to the extent of the Group’s             確認已轉讓資產。就此而言，本集團亦將
  continuing involvement. In that case, the Group also recognises          確認相關負債。已轉讓資產及相關負債的

  Continuing involvement that takes the form of a guarantee over the       持續參與指就已轉讓資產作出的一項擔

  that the Group could be required to repay.


  IMPAIRMENT OF FINANCIAL ASSETS                                           金融資產減值
  The Group recognises an allowance for expected credit losses             本集團確認對並非持作以公允價值計量並
  (“ECLs”) for all debt instruments not held at fair value through         計入損益的所有債務工具的預期信貸虧損
  profit or loss. ECLs are based on the difference between the             （「預期信貸虧損」）的一項撥備。預期信貸
  contractual cash flows due in accordance with the contract and all       虧損乃基於根據合同到期的合同現金流量
  the cash flows that the Group expects to receive, discounted at an       與本集團預期收取的所有現金流量之間的
  approximation of the original effective interest rate. The expected      差額而釐定，並以原實際利率的近似值折
  cash flows will include cash flows from the sale of collateral held      現。預期現金流量將包括出售所持抵押的
  or other credit enhancements that are integral to the contractual        現金流量或組成合同條款的其他信貸提升
  terms.                                                                   措施的現金流量。


  General approach                                                         一般方式
  ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit exposures for              預期信貸虧損分兩個階段確認。就初始確
  which there has not been a significant increase in credit risk since     認以來信貸風險並無大幅增加的信貸敞口

  from default events that are possible within the next 12 months (a       貸虧損）可能發生的違約事件所產生的信貸
  12-month ECL). For those credit exposures for which there has            虧損計提預期信貸虧損撥備。就初始確認
  been a significant increase in credit risk since initial recognition,    以來信貸風險大幅增加的信貸敞口而言，
  a loss allowance is required for credit losses expected over the         須就預期於敞口的餘下年期產生的信貸虧
  remaining life of the exposure, irrespective of the timing of the        損計提虧損撥備，不論違約的時間（年限內
  default (a lifetime ECL).                                                預期信貸虧損）。

      information.

      available without undue cost or effort. In making that evaluation,       是否被視為具有低信貸風險。於評估時，

      has been a significant increase in credit risk when contractual          本集團認為信貸風險大幅上升。
      payments are past due.

      payments are 90 days past due. However, in certain cases,                為金融資產違約。然而，在若干情況下，

      before taking into account any credit enhancements held by the           可認為金融資產違約。倘無法合理預期收
      Group. A financial asset is written off when there is no reasonable      回合同現金流量，則撇銷金融資產。

  GOVERNMENT GRANTS                                                       政府補貼
  Government grants are recognised at their fair value where there        政府補貼是在合理確保將可收取補貼及符
  is reasonable assurance that the grant will be received and all         合所有附帶條件時按其公允價值予以確
  attaching conditions will be complied with. When the grant relates      認。倘補貼與一項開支項目有關，則該等
  to an expense item, it is recognised as income on a systematic          補貼於擬用作補償的成本支出期間內按系
  basis over the periods that the costs, for which it is intended to      統基礎確認為收入。
  compensate, are expensed.


  Where the grant relates to an asset, the fair value is credited to a    倘補貼與一項資產有關，則其公允價值會
  deferred income account and is released to the statement of profit      計入遞延損益賬，並按均等年度分期，於
  or loss over the expected useful life of the relevant asset by equal    有關資產的預期可使用年期調撥至損益
  annual instalments or deducted from the carrying amount of the          表，或自該項資產的賬面值中扣除並透過
  asset and released to the statement of profit or loss by way of a       減少折舊開支方式調撥至損益表。
  reduced depreciation charge.


  REVENUE RECOGNITION                                                     收入確認
  Revenue from contracts with customers                                   客戶合同收入
  Revenue from contracts with customers is recognised when                當貨品或服務控制權按反映本集團預期有
  control of goods or services is transferred to the customers at an      權獲得以交換該等貨品或服務的代價金額
  amount that reflects the consideration to which the Group expects       轉移至客戶時，確認客戶合同收入。
  to be entitled in exchange for those goods or services.


  The Group sells products to a customer who is also the supplier         本集團向特定客戶銷售商品，該客戶同時
  of key materials used in the manufacturing of products. The Group       也是商品製造過程中使用的關鍵材料的供
  obtains the control of the materials purchased from the customer        應商。本集團取得自客戶採購的材料的控
  and provides significant services to integrate materials with           制權並提供重要服務，從而將材料與其他
  other goods and services into a portfolio of outputs. The Group         商品及服務綜合為一個產出組合。本集團
  considered itself as a principal in the arrangement and accordingly     在該安排中視其為主要責任人，因此按總
  recognises revenue on a gross basis.                                    額基準確認收入。


  (a) Sale of goods                                                       (a) 銷售商品
      Revenue from the sale of goods is recognised at the point in           來自銷售商品的收入在資產控制權轉
      time when control of the asset is transferred to the customer,         移至客戶時（一般為交接該等商品時）
      generally on delivery and acceptance of the goods.                     確認。



  (b) Provision for services                                              (b) 提供服務

      scheduled period on an input method because the customer               期間確認，因為客戶在收到本集團提

      by the Group.

 3. SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING JUDGEMENTS AND                                       3. 主要會計判斷及估計
    ESTIMATES
      The preparation of the Group’s financial statements requires               編製本集團的財務報表要求管理層作出影
      management to make judgements, estimates and assumptions                   響收入、開支、資產及負債的報告金額及
      that affect the reported amounts of revenues, expenses, assets             其附帶披露以及或然負債披露的判斷、估
      and liabilities, and their accompanying disclosures, and the               計及假設。該等假設及估計並不明確，可
      disclosure of contingent liabilities. Uncertainty about these              能會導致出現需要對將來受影響的資產或
      assumptions and estimates could result in outcomes that could              負債的賬面值作出重大調整。
      require a material adjustment to the carrying amounts of the
      assets or liabilities affected in the future.


      JUDGEMENTS                                                                 判斷
      In the process of applying the Group’s accounting policies,                於應用本集團會計政策的過程中，除涉及
      management has made the following judgements, apart from                   對財務報表內已確認金額構成最重大影響
      those involving estimations, which have the most significant effect        的估計項目外，管理層已作出以下判斷：
      on the amounts recognised in the financial statements:


      Principal versus agent considerations                                      主要責任人與代理人考慮事項
      The Group sells products to a customer who is also the supplier            本集團向特定客戶銷售商品，該客戶同時
      of key materials used in the manufacturing of products. The                也是商品製造過程中使用的關鍵材料的供
      Group needs to determine whether it is a principal or an agent             應商。本集團需要通過評估其對客戶的承
      in these transactions by evaluating the nature of its promise to           諾的性質來判斷其在該類交易中是主要責
      the customer. The Group is a principal and therefore records               任人還是代理人。倘本集團於向客戶轉讓
      revenue on a gross basis if it controls promised goods before              商品前控制所承諾商品，則為主要責任
      transferring the goods to the customer. Otherwise, the Group is            人，故須按總額基準記錄收入。否則，
      an agent and records as revenue the net amount that it retains for         本集團為代理人，將收入列為就其代理服
      its agency services if its role is to arrange to provide the goods.        務而保留的淨額（倘其責任為安排提供商
      To assess whether the Group controls the goods before they are             品）。為評估本集團是否在商品轉移給客戶
      transferred to the customer, the Group has considered various              之前控制商品，本集團已考慮各種因素，
      factors, including but not limited to whether the Group is (i) the         包括但不限於本集團是否 (i) 是交易安排中
      primary obligor in the arrangement, (ii) has general inventory             的主要責任人，(ii)承擔存貨的一般風險，
      risk, (iii) has latitude in establishing the selling price and (iv) has    (iii)有權自主決定售價和(iv)對商品的產出和
      significant involvement in the determination of product and service        服務規範有重大投入。
      specifications.


      Deferred tax on withholding tax arising from the distributions of          分派股息產生的預扣稅遞延稅
      dividends
      The Group’s determination as to whether to accrue for withholding          本集團乃根據派付股息的時間判斷釐定是

  to measure lease liabilities. The IBR is the rate of interest that the    利 率 」）計 量 租 賃 負 債 。 增 量 借 款 利 率 為
  Group would have to pay to borrow over a similar term, and with a         本集團於類似經濟環境中為取得與使用權
  similar security, the funds necessary to obtain an asset of a similar     資產價值相近之資產，而以類似抵押品於
  value to the right-of-use asset in a similar economic environment.        類似期間借入所需資金應支付之利率。因
  The IBR therefore reflects what the Group “would have to                  此，增量借款利率反映了本集團「應支付」
  pay”, which requires estimation when no observable rates are              的利率，當無可觀察的利率時（如就並無訂

  the subsidiary’s functional currency). The Group estimates the            則須作出利率估計。當可觀察輸入數據可

 14. LEASES                                                                           14. 租賃
      THE GROUP AS A LESSEE                                                                本集團作為承租人
      The Group has lease contracts for various items of buildings,                        本集團擁有多個用於其經營活動中的樓
      leasehold land, machinery and other equipment used in its                            宇、租賃土地、機器及其他設備項目的租
      operations. Lump sum payments were made upfront to acquire                           賃合同。向土地擁有人收購租賃土地前已
      the leased land from the owners with lease periods of 44 to 99                       作出一次性付款，租賃期為44 至 99 年，
      years, and no ongoing payments will be made under the terms                          且根據該等土地租賃條款並無正在進行的
      of these land leases. Leases of buildings generally have lease                       款項。樓宇租賃一般擁有 1 至 20 年的租賃
      terms between 1 and 20 years. Machinery and other equipment                          期。機器及其他設備一般擁有一至五年的
      generally have lease terms of 1 to 5 years and/or are individually                   租賃期及 ╱ 或單個設備價值較低。一般而
      of low value. Generally, the Group is restricted from assigning and                  言，本集團受限制於在集團外部轉撥或分
      subleasing the leased assets outside the Group. There are several                    租租賃資產。多項租賃合同包含延期選擇
      lease contracts that include extension options, which are further                    權，下文將對其進一步討論。
      discussed below.

  provision matrix to measure expected credit losses. The provision       析，以計量預期信貸虧損。撥備率基於具
  rates are based on days past due for groupings of various               有類似虧損模式的各客戶群按產品類型分

  The calculation reflects the probability-weighted outcome, the          果、貨幣時間價值及於報告日可獲得的有
  time value of money and reasonable and supportable information          關過去事件、當前狀況及未來經濟狀況預
  that is available at the reporting date about past events, current      測的合理可支持性資料。一般而言，應收
  conditions and forecasts of future economic conditions. Generally,      貿易賬款如逾期超過一年且不受執法活動
  trade receivables are written off if past due for more than one year    影響，則予以撇銷。
  and are not subject to enforcement activity.

 29. CONTINGENT LIABILITIES                                                     29. 或然負債
      ACTION AGAINST FOXCONN                                                      富士康訴訟案件
      On 11 June 2007, a Hong Kong High Court action (the “June                   於二零零七年六月十一日，富士康國際控
      2007 Action”) was commenced by a subsidiary and an affiliate of             股有限公司旗下一間附屬公司及一間聯屬
      Foxconn International Holdings Limited (the “Plaintiffs”) against the       公 司（「 原 告 」）向 香 港 高 等 法 院 展 開 訴 訟
      Company and certain subsidiaries of the Group (the “Defendants”)           （「二零零七年六月訴訟」），指控本公司及
      for using confidential information alleged to have been obtained            本集團若干附屬公司（「被告」）使用指稱自
      improperly from the Plaintiffs. The Plaintiffs discontinued the June        原告處非法獲得的機密資料。隨著針對被
      2007 Action on 5 October 2007 with the effect that the June 2007            告的二零零七年六月訴訟被全面撤銷以及
      Action has been wholly discontinued against all the Defendants              該訴訟未判令被告承擔任何責任，原告已
      named in the action and this finally disposed of the June 2007              於二零零七年十月五日停止二零零七年六
      Action without any liability to the Defendants. On the same                 月訴訟。同日，原告向法院提起新一輪的
      day, the Plaintiffs initiated a new set of legal proceedings in the         法律程序（「二零零七年十月訴訟」）。二零
      Court (the “October 2007 Action”). The Defendants named in the              零七年十月訴訟的被告與二零零七年六月
      October 2007 Action are the same as the Defendants in the June              訴訟的被告相同，且原告在二零零七年十
      2007 Action, and the claims made by the Plaintiffs in the October           月訴訟中提出的申索均基於二零零七年六
      2007 Action are based on the same facts and the same grounds                月訴訟中的相同事實及理由。原告在二零
      in the June 2007 Action. The remedies sought by the Plaintiffs              零七年十月訴訟中提出的補救方法包括強
      in the October 2007 Action include an injunction restraining the            令禁止被告使用有關機密資料、強令被告
      Defendants from using the alleged confidential information, an              交出因使用機密資料所獲得的利潤以及賠
      order for the disgorgement of profit made by the Defendants                 償原告遭受的損失及支付懲罰性賠償金。
      through the use of the confidential information, damages based              原告在二零零七年十月訴訟中主張的全部
      on the loss suffered by the Plaintiffs and exemplary damages. The           賠償金數額尚未確定。
      total damages sought by the Plaintiffs in the October 2007 Action
      have not been quantified.


      On 2 October 2009, the Defendants instituted a counter-action               於二零零九年十月二日，被告對富士康國
      against Foxconn International Holdings Limited and certain of its           際控股有限公司及其若干聯屬公司利用不
      affiliates for their intervention, by means of illegal measures, in the     合法手段干涉本公司及其若干附屬公司的
      operations involving the Company and certain of its subsidiaries,           經營、共謀行為、書面及口頭誹謗，導致
      collusions, written and verbal defamation, and the economic loss            經濟損失的行為提起反訴。
      as a result of the said activities.


      As at the reporting date, the case remains in the process of                於本報告日期，該訴訟案仍處於法律訴訟
      legal proceedings. After consulting the Company’s legal counsel             階段。經諮詢於案件中代表本公司的本公
      representing the Company for the case, the board is of the view             司法律顧問，董事會認為直至目前為止尚
      that the estimate of ultimate outcome and amount to settle the              難以可靠估計該訴訟的最終結果及了結訴
      obligation, if any, of the litigation cannot be made reliably up to         訟須支付的有關款項金額（如有）。
      date.

  operations.

      The Group has transactional currency exposures. Such exposures                        本集團有交易貨幣風險。有關風險來自經
      arise from sales or purchases by operating units in currencies                        營單位以並非該單位的功能貨幣進行的買

      in currencies other than the RMB. The Group tends to accept                           立買賣合同時接納避免外匯風險或分配條
      foreign currency exchange risk avoidance or allocation terms                          款。本集團對外匯收入及開支實行滾動預
      when arriving at purchase and sale contracts. The Group takes                         測，將貨幣與費用金額匹配，以減輕匯率

      matches the currency and the amount incurred so as to alleviate

      CAPITAL MANAGEMENT                                                                       資本管理
      The primary objectives of the Group’s capital management are to                          本集團資本管理的主要目標為確保本集團
      safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern and                         持續發展的能力及保持良好的資本比率，
      to maintain healthy capital ratios in order to support its business                      以支持本集團的業務及盡量提升股東價值。
      and maximise shareholders’ value.


      The Group manages its capital structure and makes adjustments                            本集團根據經濟狀況的變化管理其資本架
      to it in light of changes in economic conditions. To maintain or                         構及作出調整。為保持或調整資本架構，
      adjust the capital structure, the Group may adjust the dividend                          本集團或會調整派付予股東的股息、股
      payment to shareholders, return capital to shareholders or issue                         東的資本回報或發行新股。截至二零二
      new shares. No changes were made in the objectives, policies                             零年十二月三十一日及二零一九年十二
